To get the **second Bow Switch Skill 3, Aerial Aim, you must upgrade or forge eight weapons for the Bow then go to talk to Master Utsushi in the Gathering Hub.** It may be best to upgrade/forge in the **Hub Prep Area** to save time when checking the Master for a **yellow** "OK, Ace! Lesson time!" prompt above his head.

Keep in mind are that you can **craft a series of five or six weapons along the Kamura, Ore and Bone Trees** while making some slightly more expensive ones as you go along to save resources and remember that the Kamura Map will not show update to show that he is available immediately after forging/upgrading - even if you reset the game.

You will find that each increasing tier of charge has more power which is standard but you can speed up the process with the Charging Evade which immediately levels up at least one level unless you become a Dodgebolt master to go even further beyond.

## Version Changes

> **Further plans include:**
>
> - Covering omitted quests (Requests and more) and clarifying weapon use.
> - Adding helpful images or videos for context where appropriate.
> - Expanding on the FAQ.
> - Separating and reorganising the information to flow logically.
>
> While I have priorities in mind, **I'm happy to consider other suggestions if you send a DM, leave feedback or simply if I come across an interesting comment or concern.** Don't worry about an update that only removes typos.

**Version 0.8 (01/06/2021) -** \[ _Move sets for each of the 14 weapons_\] **\-** Weapons, weapons, weapons! There are still many little things to cover and a decent chance that there are some errors since I'm not a specialist of every weapon, but there should be a surprise or two. The presentation is inconsistent, but if you understand the controls from one page, it should help you for another weapon especially as the real time action display doesn't apply for two of the long range weapons.

If you were hoping to see 2.0.0 content get covered in full or even some Ver 3.0.0 stuff, that will probably come with time. Quests are still bundled together in a single place, but that'll change in future I imagine.

* * *

**Version 0.7 (13/05/2021) -**\[7 _★ for hub complete (blue tick) & 6★ village complete plus a bit more (167 quests)_【HR 20】\] **-** Rather than end on the high note that was HR8, it felt too natural to reach Hunter Rank 20 since Hub Progress gates it and it can be used as a way to structure updates. This doesn´t necessarily mean that all updates from here on out will be focused on these core quests since I´ve got enough notes for Requests to get a lot done, there are Weapons in this game or so I heard and then there´s the Dango...

I can´t say I was expecting all of the typos to be high priorities or that there wouldn´t be company for **Dango Defender** in the game updates, but seeing **four quests in a row with mission critical information being incorrect** was a shock since I hadn´t needed to contradict the game on purpose besides one gathering quest IIRC. My plan is to leave minor ones like quest names alone if the exact phrasing will be searched by users, but I can´t leave incorrect target monsters (someone likes to "Hunt a Royal Ludroth and a Jyuratodus") or missing environment data alone even if **I do care about typos (my own)**.

I think it is safe to assume that a lot of these typos are here to stay like the Devil May Cry 4's "Misson Menu", but I'd like to get more funny ones or to hear about what mistakes or misconceptions result from the other translations of the game. It isn't alien for a Japanese game to have a typo in its original language either, but it has been a great ride regardless since it isn't Ace Attorney I suppose.

* * *

**Version 0.6 (04/05/2021) -**\[6 _★ for hub complete & most (?) of 6★ village plus a bit more (144 quests)_\] **-** A pretty straightforward update since it turns out that there wasn't too much that could be implemented from the Digital Event and the Ver. 2.0.0 besides **a mention in the questions area for the Character Edit Voucher** (hopefully won't be needed in Stories 2, like the original). Both rampages and weapons are moving up in the priorities since there isn't too much else to cover related to the 2.0.0 update and it seems that major changes will probably be a little while away.

As for additional rambles, it is tempting to talk about the initial 7★ hunt some more but I'm going to talk about GameFAQs for a bit instead. The beta is promising for how it streamlines access but has some implications with older links (not an issue for when "gamespot" joined the URL IIRC) or for archiving in general, but nothing bad enough that a bit of planning from users can't get around. The only thing that sticks out which doesn't seem to be a setting is making the platform more obvious during searches (it is fine for forums - can click a filter). It would just help when there are a lot of arcade vs console differences making the similarities more in name only, though I admit I don't know enough about the whole Bionic Commando series (GB game is the best so far) to say that's how Capcom rolled. Oldheads may know that Mega Drive and SNES versions can be very different as well, so the differences are pretty subtle nowadays though underreported all the same.

In any case, things are looking great if you don't want to check multiple boards for discussions (it'll be appreciated for Resident Evil 4), not so great if you want to having the illusion of claiming a board based on a niche version with a slow thread that reaches 500 after years.

* * *

**Version 0.5 (27/04/2021) -**\[ _5★ for both village and hub complete plus a bit more (122 quests), basic controls implemented_\] **\-** Between having a weak guide for all weapons and a decent guide for a few weapons, I chose neither... My most played games are usually fighting games of all kinds and what some people call "character action" games, so I had a lot of ideas about notation (Normal, Special and Unique are official terms) and the kind of language I want to bring to the table, but I'm scaling it back to focus on the basics so the weapon guide versions will need to be changed less (at least from a reader's perspective). Ultimately, I think I will need the PC version of the game to be fully happy with how I can display weapon information in my style, but I'm moving at the least. **Kudos to Etner for putting in work for Charge Blade hunters!**

Other thoughts? I guess I can say that this guide is a return to my roots since there are over 100 images (not all are in use) but none are screenshots of direct gameplay so far. Normally, I try to write without giving too much bias if there are a lot of ways to play and Monster Hunter is packed with things to use. This doesn't mean that I don't capture thousands of images or video to use as reference or editing, but it is a blessing that I'm not doing a quest by quest obligation to have perfect images since I'd never be done. Whether it is the simple cropped placeholder image to represent random monsters or the small but edited control icons, I've tried to reduce friction since I'd never stop if I actually made this into **Monster Hunter Snap**. Maybe if the Photo Mode was more powerful like in Mortal Shell...

Lastly, this guide update was finished before the Monster Hunter Digital Event, so I can't add new specifics on why I'm really looking forward to Monster Hunter Stories 2 (without leaks anyway) but the Stories subseries has the same wyvern riding gameplay but monsters that actually want you there! Anyway, I'm sure a lot of readers are more interested in action games, but I recommend that you give the original game (mobile and 3DS) a shot and **keep an eye on the sequel at least for the music**. On the seemingly unrelated note of Smash Bros, if you think a generic playable Hunter would be cool... A Rider and Monstie combo (Lute + Ratha, skins for other folks) would be **way past cool**.

* * *

**Version 0.4 (22/04/2021) -** \[4 _★ for both village and hub complete and a bit more, 96 quests_\] **\-** No weapons yet... Mainly due to some structural problems with presenting the information and not wanting to leave any weapons behind (Prowlers don't count), given that there are bigger issues with the quests which are due for two pages at a minimum. It is probably for the best to stay focused and build up gradually... Will I go beyond 100 and cover the weapons at last in the next one? You'll find out next week in game magazines...

As far as the game impressions, besides Magnamalo in the Village Quest line, things really slowed down but it was an interesting challenge to write the scaled-back material (especially the Master Utsushi ones) while focusing on High Rank differences. I'm certain that I've built up a few errors but a lot of them are hopefully only in my scraps which are filtered before you get the newer drafts. It shouldn't be anything that a bit of Whetstone and feedback can't fix.

* * *

**Version 0.3 (13/04/2021) -**\[3 _★ for both village and hub complete and a bit more, 69 quests_\] **-** Nice to be moving into HR4... High Rank... While I'm also going through a milestone IRL, it isn't one that I expect to stop the guide from coming along. Weapons should be included in the next one, but I really expect details to be scaled back so I can scaffold it into the form I want without worrying too much about big reforms (which is why Rampage Quests, in particular, are absent).

While the village quests were more of the same, the climax of the hub quests is really something between the Apex Arzuros's rampage and a clutch save for the Disastrously Beautiful Endurance quest since I didn't follow my own advice by bringing spare trap materials. I'm aware that some of my older material for the first and second-star quests are phrased in a clunky way, but it'll all shape up in due time and is functional at the least. I tend to make improved versions that are tailored if a monster appears Village quests later especially if the context changes but I intend things to go further in the end. This may mean that text gets moved somewhere else rather than becoming bulkier.

* * *

**Version 0.2 (06/04/2021) -**\[ _2★ for both village and hub complete and a bit more, 39 quests_\] \- Now we are moving into HR3, but I actually made some small removals with some being temporary like the quest numbers (due to be revised when the guide is closer to being final). The rampage will have to be put back for a bit so it can be both presentable and helpful when it arrives as well.

Magmamalo was pretty interesting since I noticed some behaviour that would have been too much to mention for a single quest rather than a boss guide or monster entry (those are coming in due time - probably going to move lots of info around). One highlight is how it licks itself like a cat when it isn't feeding or on the prowl, it caught me off guard and probably others who may be on edge expecting it to explode at any time.

* * *

**Version 0.1 (30/03/2021)** \- \[ _1★ for both village and hub complete and a bit more, 18 quests_\] \- This first draft is a vertical slice that has some inconsistent formatting, but should make it clear where I'm going to some extent. I would have liked it to cover weapons, but this is effective for establishing a structure that I can revise with time while weapons will need some time to be covered in a way that does them justice. Top-notch dangos aren't built in a day... and wonderful walkthroughs aren't built in a weekend, y'know.

My thoughts on the game itself? Really fun and great at rewarding you for just looking whether it is interacting with the people of Kamura between quests even without prompts, climbing to the top of a tree in the Buddy Plaza just to see what's there or getting multiple goals done during a Quest - perhaps during the final timer after being successful.

- [Next: Quests](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/switch/300976-monster-hunter-rise/faqs/79167/quests)

## Privacy Preference Center

When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences, or your device, and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect. The information does not usually identify you directly, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. Click on the different category headings to learn more and change our default settings. Blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience of the site and the services we are able to offer.


Allow All

### Manage Consent Preferences

#### Strictly Necessary Cookies

Always Active

These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.

#### Performance Cookies

Always Active

These cookies allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. All information these cookies collect is aggregated and therefore anonymous. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site, and will not be able to monitor its performance.

#### Functional Cookies

Always Active

These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.

#### Targeting Cookies

Always Active

These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.

#### Social Media Cookies

Always Active

These cookies are set by a range of social media services that we have added to the site to enable you to share our content with your friends and networks. They are capable of tracking your browser across other sites and building up a profile of your interests. This may impact the content and messages you see on other websites you visit. If you do not allow these cookies you may not be able to use or see these sharing tools.
